Seated as claims DV11-01..05: the discipline your children have not seen: love the lord your god and keep his charge, his statutes, his judgments and his commandments always; know today — not your children who have not known and have not seen the discipline of the lord your god, his greatness, his mighty hand and his outstretched arm — his signs and deeds in egypt to pharaoh; what he did to egypt's army, its horses and chariots, over whom he made the waters of the red sea flow as they pursued you, and the lord destroyed them to this day; what he did for you in the wilderness until you came to this place; and what he did to dathan and abiram, sons of eliab son of reuben — the earth opened its mouth and swallowed them, their households, their tents and every living thing at their feet, in the midst of all israel; for your eyes have seen every great deed of the lord which he did; the land watered by heaven: keep all the commandment which i command you today, that you may be strong and go in and possess the land you are crossing to possess, and prolong days on the land the lord swore to your fathers to give to them and to their seed, a land flowing with milk and honey; for the land you are entering to possess is not like the land of egypt from which you came out, where you sowed your seed and watered it with your foot like a garden of herbs — the land you are crossing to possess is a land of hills and valleys, drinking water by the rain of heaven, a land the lord your god cares for; the eyes of the lord your god are always on it, from the beginning of the year to the end of the year; the second paragraph — the rain conditional, the frontlets, the doorposts: if you hearken, hearken to my commandments which i command you today, to love the lord your god and serve him with all your heart and all your soul, i will give the rain of your land in its season, the early rain and the late rain, and you shall gather your grain, your wine and your oil; i will give grass in your field for your cattle, and you shall eat and be satisfied; take heed to yourselves lest your heart be deceived and you turn aside and serve other gods and bow down to them, and the anger of the lord be kindled against you and he shut the heavens and there be no rain and the ground not give its produce, and you perish quickly from off the good land the lord gives you; put these my words on your heart and on your soul, bind them for a sign on your hand and let them be frontlets between your eyes; teach them to your sons, speaking of them sitting in your house, walking on the way, lying down and rising; write them on the doorposts of your house and on your gates — that your days and your sons' days be multiplied on the land the lord swore to your fathers to give to them, as the days of the heavens above the earth; the borders and the dread: if you keep, keep all this commandment which i command you to do it — to love the lord your god, to walk in all his ways and to cleave to him — the lord will dispossess all these nations from before you, and you shall dispossess nations greater and mightier than you; every place where the sole of your foot treads shall be yours — from the wilderness and lebanon, from the river, the river euphrates, to the western sea shall be your border; no man shall stand before you: the lord your god will put the dread of you and the fear of you on the face of all the land you tread, as he spoke to you; THE LAND WATERED BY HEAVEN: KEEP ALL THE COMMANDMENT WHICH I COMMAND YOU TODAY, THAT YOU MAY BE STRONG AND GO IN AND POSSESS THE LAND YOU ARE CROSSING TO POSSESS, AND PROLONG DAYS ON THE LAND THE LORD SWORE TO YOUR FATHERS TO GIVE TO THEM AND TO THEIR SEED, A LAND FLOWING WITH MILK AND HONEY; FOR THE LAND YOU ARE ENTERING TO POSSESS IS NOT LIKE THE LAND OF EGYPT FROM WHICH YOU CAME OUT, WHERE YOU SOWED YOUR SEED AND WATERED IT WITH YOUR FOOT LIKE A GARDEN OF HERBS — THE LAND YOU ARE CROSSING TO POSSESS IS A LAND OF HILLS AND VALLEYS, DRINKING WATER BY THE RAIN OF HEAVEN, A LAND THE LORD YOUR GOD CARES FOR; THE EYES OF THE LORD YOUR GOD ARE ALWAYS ON IT, FROM THE BEGINNING OF THE YEAR TO THE END OF THE YEAR [claim DV11-02]. THE LAND WATERED BY HEAVEN: KEEP ALL THE COMMANDMENT WHICH I COMMAND YOU TODAY, THAT YOU MAY BE STRONG AND GO IN AND POSSESS THE LAND YOU ARE CROSSING TO POSSESS, AND PROLONG DAYS ON THE LAND THE LORD SWORE TO YOUR FATHERS TO GIVE TO THEM AND TO THEIR SEED, A LAND FLOWING WITH MILK AND HONEY; FOR THE LAND YOU ARE ENTERING TO POSSESS IS NOT LIKE THE LAND OF EGYPT FROM WHICH YOU CAME OUT, WHERE YOU SOWED YOUR SEED AND WATERED IT WITH YOUR FOOT LIKE A GARDEN OF HERBS — THE LAND YOU ARE CROSSING TO POSSESS IS A LAND OF HILLS AND VALLEYS, DRINKING WATER BY THE RAIN OF HEAVEN, A LAND THE LORD YOUR GOD CARES FOR; THE EYES OF THE LORD YOUR GOD ARE ALWAYS ON IT, FROM THE BEGINNING OF THE YEAR TO THE END OF THE YEAR.
